Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on networks structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 104. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 581 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the networks space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 104 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 581. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the networks structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 54. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
